Let's refine log-LSE optimizer function `optimize_log_lbfgs(a_start, deadline, betas, maxfun_per_stage, label)`.
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_log_lbfgs(a_start, deadline, betas, maxfun, label):
    a = normalize_a(a_start)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300) + ?)  # if zeros, use floor relative
    best_z=z.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a)
    for beta in betas:
       sub_dead = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+ budget)
       while time<sub_dead and maxfun remaining:
           res = minimize(lse_log_value_grad, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(beta,), options=...)
           a_candidate=softmax(res.x)
           sc=exact_score_a(a_candidate)
           if sc<best_sc: update
           z=normalize_log(res.x)
    return best_sc,best_a
```

`lse_log_value_grad(z, beta)`:
```
a, zc = softmax_z(z)
n=a.size
S=a.sum() #1
c=fast_self_conv(a)
d=n*c/(S*S)
dmax=d.max(); if dmax<=0 return large zeros
zeta=beta*(d-dmax); clip to [-50,50]
e=np.exp(zeta); sum_e=e.sum()
w=e/sum_e
L=dmax + np.log(sum_e)/beta
h=corr_pair(a,w)
LDS=float(np.dot(w,d))
# score = 2*L + pen*(S-1)^2 maybe pen=0 in log because S=1
score=2.0*L
grad=(4.0*n*h)/(S*S) - (4.0*LDS)/S
# grad for score wrt a
gm=float(np.dot(a,grad))
gz = a*(grad - gm)
gz = nan_to_num, clip
return score,gz
```

`mirror_descent_log(a, beta_w, eta, k, tol)` returns candidate.
Pseudo:
```
def mirror_log_step(a, w, alpha):
    d = n*c
    S=1
    h=corr_pair(a,w)
    LDS=dot(w,d)
    grad=(4n h)/S^2 -4LDS/S
    p=grad - dot(a,grad)  # constant removed
    scale=max(abs(p))
    if scale==0 return a
    s=p/scale
    a_new=a*np.exp(-alpha*s)
    a_new[~finite]=0
    return normalize_a(a_new)
```
Actually if using log step, z_i = log a_i, derivative projected p_i, update z_i <- z_i - eta p_i/max_scale. So a_new = a exp(-eta p/scale). Good.

Line search alpha in [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1,2]. Keep best.

p=p/scale

```

Pseudo:
```
def refine_with_beta(a, beta, deadline, maxfun, label):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    best_a = a.copy(); best_sc = exact_score_a(a)
    z, a_z = init_z(a)
    bounds=make_bounds(a.size,-70,70)
    cps=50.0
    stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 3:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5: break
        mf = int(min(maxfun, max(64, cps*rem*0.8)))
        tmc = time.monotonic()
        try:
            res = minimize(lse_log_value_grad, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', args=(beta,), bounds=bounds, options={'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxiter':mf,'maxfun':mf,'maxls':20,'maxcor':30})
        except Exception:
            break
        dt = time.monotonic()-tmc
        nfev = int(getattr(res,'nfev', mf) or mf)
        if dt>1e-3: cps = max(10, min(2000, 0.7*cps +0.3*nfev/dt)) else pass
        z_new = getattr(res,'x', z)
        if z_new is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(z_new)): break
        a_new=softmax_z(z_new)[0]
        sc=exact_score_a(a_new)
        improved = np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc
        if improved:
            best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new.copy()
            stall=0
        else:
            stall+=1
        z=z_new
        a=a_new
        if getattr(res,'status',0)==1: break
        if stall >= 5 and res.success: break
    return a, best_a, best_sc
```
But exact score after each L-BFGS call uses FFT. L-BFGS score is LSE; we can also compare LSE? exact ok. If a_new is NaN, break.

`softmax_z` top-level:
```
def softmax_z(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   if z.size==0 return empty
   z=z - np.mean(z)
   # optionally clip for safety: if z.max()>700? bounds prevent, but if no: z = np.clip(z,-700,700)
   e=np.exp(z)
   S=e.sum()
   if not finite or S<=0: return normalized ones? 
   a=e/S
   return a, z
```
If z has huge due bounds? no.

Let's examine if log-LSE `lse_log_value_grad` may be too slow due `corr_pair` for each w: In LSE, w size 2n-1 and `corr_pair` uses `rfft(w)` each call. That's extra FFT of length N (N=next power M=n+wsize-1? Wait for corr_pair w length 2n-1, M= n+2n-2=3n-2, N next power (e.g. for n=12288, M=36862, N=65536). That's larger than self conv N=32768. corr_pair FFT length N=65536, more expensive. self conv N=32768. So each LSE fun: self conv rfft 32768 + irfft 32768; corr rfft(a_rev) 65536, rfft(w) 65536, irfft 65536. Heavy! Old program used same. Each fun maybe ~0.01-0.02s. 3000 fun ~30-60s per beta. Could be too slow. Need optimize gradient computation.

Can we compute gradient more efficiently? For LSE weighted gradient h = half derivative = cross-correlation. We can compute via FFT length M (next pow for n + wsize -1) -> 65536 for 12k. self conv length 2n -> 32768. This is okay but 5 FFTs (some length 65k). 30k calls maybe high. Need ensure within time. For n=10240, M=30719, N=32768; corr length 32768, self N=32768? self M=20479, N=32768. Both 32768. Better! n_primary 10240 reduces corr N to 32768. n=12288 corr N=65536 (2x). Maybe choose n=10240 or 10239? Use 10240 as previous. Since parent length 10240. For n=10240, self conv M=20479 next power 32768; corr M=10240+20479-1=30718 next power 32768. Good. Use n_primary=10240! Larger 12288 doubles corr cost. Hidden eval 10240 okay. Finite error 1/10240 small. Let's choose 10240. Final try 12288 maybe but corr 65k slower. Could restrict final to 10240. Previous target 1.5027 maybe reachable at 10240. Use 10240 for primary and final. Good.

What about small n=1024, corr N=4096? M=3071 ->4096, self 2047->2048. Fast.

Potential optimization: Compute h via linear convolution using `np.convolve`? No. Could precompute inverse FFT of a? Not for varying a. Could compute gradient using `np.convolve(a, w, mode='full')` with w? O(n^2) impossible. FFT best.

Let's implement:
```
def corr_pair(a,w):
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return empty
    if w.size==0: return zeros(n)
    wmax=float(np.max(np.abs(w)))
    if wmax <=0: return zeros(n)
    th = 1e-10 * wmax  # small
    idx = np.nonzero(np.abs(w) > th)[0]
    if idx.size <= 200:
       h=np.zeros(n); for l in idx: wt=float(w[l]); ...
       return h
    # else FFT
    M=n + w.size -1; N=next_power_of_two(M); A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N); W=np.fft.rfft(w,N); full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]; return full[n-1:2n-1]
```

Detailed function implementations:

1. `next_power_of_two(m)`:
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
If m=1, bit_length(0)? m-1=0 bit_length=0 =>1. good.

2. `normalize_a(a, target=1.0)`:
```
def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([],float)
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-15:
       return np.full(n, target/n if n>0 else 0.0)
    return a*(target/S)
```

3. `resample_sequence(a,n)`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); m=a.size; n=int(max(1,n))
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if m==n: return a.astype(float)
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```

4. `fast_self_conv(a)`:
```
def fast_self_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    M=2*n-1; N=next_power_of_two(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
    out=out[:M]
    out=np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
    return out
```
The `np.maximum` may hide negative artifacts.

5. `corr_pair(a,w)`:
```
def corr_pair(a,w):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); w=np.asarray(w,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    if w.size==0: return np.zeros(n)
    wmax=float(np.max(np.abs(w)))
    if wmax <= 1e-300: return np.zeros(n)
    th=1e-9*wmax
    idx=np.nonzero(np.abs(w)>th)[0]
    if idx.size >0 and idx.size <= 64:
       h=np.zeros(n)
       for l in idx:
          wt=float(w[l])
          i0 = l - (n-1)
          if i0 < 0: i0=0
          i1 = l
          if i1 > n-1: i1 = n-1
          if i0 > i1: continue
          j0 = l - i0
          j1 = l - i1
          # j0 >= j1
          if j0 < 0 or j1 >= n: continue
          j0 = min(j0, n-1); j1 = max(j1,0)
          h[i0:i1+1] += wt * a[j1:j0+1][::-1]
       return h
    M = n + w.size - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full=np.fft.irfft(A*W, N)
    full=full[:M]
    return full[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need if idx.size=0? then zero. If idx size >64 use FFT. But if w has many small weights but broad, FFT. Good.
Potential issue: In sparse, if `idx` includes l outside 0..2n-2? w should length; if l<0/l large, slice handles. `j0=min...` after i; okay.

But if w has max very small? fine.

6. `d_from_a(a)`:
```
def d_from_a(a):
   a=...; n; S=sum; if S<1e-12 return zeros, S
   c=fast_self_conv(a); d=n*c/(S*S); return d,S
```

7. `exact_score_a(a)` similar.

8. `make_bounds(n, lo, hi)` returns `[(lo,hi)]*n` but if n=0 empty.

9. `softmax_from_u(u)`:
```
def softmax_from_u(u):
   u=np.asarray(u,float).ravel()
   n=u.size+1
   z=np.empty(n); z[0]=0.0; z[1:]=u
   z=np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=80.0, neginf=-80.0)
   m=z.max()
   e=np.exp(z-m)
   S=e.sum()
   if not finite or S<=0: return np.full(n,1/n)
   return e/S
```
If u contains 700, nan_to_num posinf only; but `z-m` finite; exponential overflow if m large? m could be 1e308 if u finite huge? L-BFGS bounds prevent. We can clip `z=np.clip(z,-80,80)` before m; but clipping changes if >80? Bounds. Use clip for safety: `z=np.clip(z,-80,80)`. But if z0=0 and some z=80, okay. If z=-80, exp(-80). Good.

10. `init_u_from_a(a)`:
```
def init_u_from_a(a):
    a=normalize_a(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<=1: return np.empty(0)
    a=np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a=a/a.sum()
    denom=a[0]
    if denom < 1e-300: denom=1e-300
    u=np.log(a[1:]/denom)
    u=np.nan_to_num(u, nan=0.0, posinf=80.0, neginf=-80.0)
    return np.clip(u, -80.0, 80.0)
```
If a[0]=1e-12, a[1]~1/n=1e-4, log 18. Good.

11. `lse_log_value_grad_u(u, beta)`:
```
def lse_log_value_grad_u(u, beta):
   u=np.asarray(u,float).ravel()
   if u.size==0: return 2.0, np.empty(0)
   n=u.size+1
   a=softmax_from_u(u)
   S=float(a.sum()) #1
   c=fast_self_conv(a)
   if S<1e-12: return 1e10, zeros
   d=n*c/(S*S)
   dmax=float(np.max(d))
   if dmax<=0 or not finite: return 1e10, zeros
   z=beta*(d-dmax)
   z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
   e=np.exp(z); sum_e=float(e.sum())
   if sum_e<=0: w=np.ones(d.size)/d.size; L=dmax
   else: w=e/sum_e; L=dmax + np.log(sum_e)/beta
   h=corr_pair(a,w)
   LDS=float(np.dot(w,d))
   score=2.0*L
   grad=(4.0*n*h)/(S*S) - (4.0*LDS)/S
   ag=float(np.dot(a,grad))
   gz=a*(grad-ag)
   g_u=gz[1:]
   g_u=np.nan_to_num(g_u, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   g_u=np.clip(g_u, -1e8, 1e8)
   return float(score), g_u
```
Issue: `L` should use sum_e after clip; if clip changed, L approximates max not exact LSE but okay. For weights, if sum_e finite. Could compute `log_sum_e` via logsumexp? Since z clipped and max 0, log sum_e ≤ log(len). Good.
If beta huge and all z clipped to -50 except max 0, sum_e ~ #ties; L approx dmax + log(ties)/beta. Good.
But `LDS` uses w and d. Good.

Potential: `score=2*L` where L=d weighted in units where dmax~score/2. For exact score=2n max c/S^2; d=n c/S^2. Max d=score/2. LSE L near max d. Score 2L. Good.

12. `refine_with_beta(a, beta, deadline, maxfun, label='')`:
Need use `minimize`. `from scipy.optimize import minimize` top. Options.
```
def refine_with_beta(a, beta, deadline, maxfun, label=''):
    a=normalize_a(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a)
    n=a.size
    if n<=1: return a,best_a,best_sc
    u=init_u_from_a(a)
    bounds=make_bounds(u.size, -80.0, 80.0)
    maxfun=int(max(64,min(4000,abs(int(maxfun)))))
    cps=80.0
    stall=0
    prev_lse=np.inf
    while True:
       now=time.monotonic(); rem=float(deadline - now)
       if rem < 5.0: break
       mf=int(min(maxfun, max(64, int(cps*rem*0.7))))
       opts={'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxiter':mf,'maxfun':mf,'maxls':20,'maxcor':30}
       tcall=time.monotonic()
       try: res=minimize(lse_log_value_grad_u, u, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(float(beta),), bounds=bounds, options=opts)
       except Exception: break
       dt=time.monotonic()-tcall
       nfev=int(getattr(res,'nfev', mf) or mf)
       if dt>1e-4: cps=max(10.0, min(2000.0, 0.6*cps+0.4*float(nfev)/dt))
       u_new=np.asarray(getattr(res,'x', u), dtype=np.float64)
       if u_new.size!=u.size or not np.all(np.isfinite(u_new)): break
       a_new=softmax_from_u(u_new)
       # If a_new sum bad, normalize? softmax sum 1 but could underflow; normalize
       a_new=normalize_a(a_new)
       sc=exact_score_a(a_new)
       lse_val=float(getattr(res,'fun', np.inf))
       improved_exact = np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-15
       if improved_exact:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new.copy(); stall=0
       else:
          stall += 1
       # For next beta/iteration: if exact improved or lse improved, continue from a_new; else maybe from best? We'll use a_new for smooth.
       u=u_new; a=a_new
       # print occasionally? Avoid too much. If label and improved: print
       if improved_exact and label:
          print(label + " beta={:.1f} improved exact={:.8f} time={:.0f}s".format(beta, best_sc, time.monotonic()-t0?)) # No global t0. Could omit.
       status=int(getattr(res,'status',0))
       success=bool(getattr(res,'success', False))
       if (success or status==1): break
       if stall >= 6: break
    return a,best_a,best_sc
```
No global t0. Print can just best. But many prints maybe okay. We can have global start? Use `TIME_START`? We need top-level no closures but can assign global? Could use `print(label, beta, sc)` without time. Or omit.

But `stall>=6` might break too early before using full sub_deadline. If no exact improvement but LSE still improving, next beta can use a_new. Fine.

However, if `res.success` true immediately because ftol at start (flat), we break and lose time for that beta. But later beta uses smooth a. Good.

`cps*rem*0.7`: For rem=600, cps=80=>33600, cap maxfun 300? We'll pass maxfun=300 primary. Good. If cap maxfun large, 33600 too high. We can also cap by fixed `max(64, min(maxfun, 1000, int(min(60, rem) * cps * 0.5)))` to avoid long single call. Simpler: `mf=int(min(maxfun, max(64, min(500, int(cps * max(1.0, rem) * 0.3)))))`. This limits to 500 fun calls. If actual cps 1000, 500 calls 0.5s. If rem 600, okay. If we need more, loop. Use cap 500 to be safe. For small n, cap 200. Define cap inside maxfun? `mf_cap = min(maxfun, int(max(64, min(700, cps*rem*0.5))))` maybe. Let's set:
```
mf = int(min(maxfun, max(64, min(700, int(cps * max(1.0, rem) * 0.5)))))
```
If cps=100, rem=60 =>3000 ->700; 700*0.005=3.5s safe. If cps=100 and fun 10ms, 7s; rem60 okay. If rem small 5s, cps100=>175, safe. Good. Use cap 700. maxfun can be 800.

13. `optimize_log_lbfgs(a_start, deadline, stages, maxfun, label)`:
```
def optimize_log_lbfgs(a_start, deadline, stages, maxfun, label=''):
   a=normalize_a(a_start)
   if a.size==0: ...
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a)
   t0=time.monotonic(); total=deadline-t0
   if total<8: return best_sc,best_a
   cum=0.0
   for beta,frac in stages:
      cum += float(frac)
      sub_dead = t0 + cum*total
      sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(deadline)-2.0)
      if sub_dead <= time.monotonic()+3: break
      a_cur, best1, best1_sc = refine_with_beta(a, float(beta), sub_dead, maxfun, label)
      if np.isfinite(best1_sc) and best1_sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=best1_sc; best_a=best1.copy()
      if np.isfinite(best1_sc):
          a=normalize_a(a_cur) # smooth
      else:
          a=normalize_a(best_a)
   return float(exact_score_a(best_a)), best_a.copy()
```
If `a_cur` is worse exact but good smooth. At end return best exact.

Stages fractions sum 1. If a stage converges early, next sub_deadline still based on total, so it gets its time. Good.

14. `quick_small_search(parent, n_small, deadline, n_out, seed)`:
```
def quick_small_search(parent, n_small, deadline, n_out, seed):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   inits=generate_inits(n_small, rng, parent, cap=20)
   scored=[]
   for idx,a in enumerate(inits):
      sc=exact_score_a(a)
      scored.append((sc, idx, a))
   scored.sort()
   n_select = min(max(1, 2*n_out), len(scored))
   top=[scored[i][2] for i in range(n_select)]
   results=[]
   total=deadline-time.monotonic()
   if total<10: return []
   per=total/float(len(top))
   small_stages=[(50.0,0.15),(200.0,0.25),(800.0,0.30),(4000.0,0.20),(16000.0,0.10)]
   for idx,a in enumerate(top):
       sub = time.monotonic() + per - 3.0
       sub = min(sub, deadline-3.0)
       sc,seq=optimize_log_lbfgs(a, sub, small_stages, maxfun=200, label='small'+str(idx))
       results.append((sc, idx, seq))
   results.sort()
   return [r[2] for r in results[:n_out]]
```
No lambda. If parent None, `generate_inits` handles.

15. `generate_inits` detailed. Use helper `prepare_init`. Need ensure not nested. Code:
```
def prepare_init(arr, n):
    if n<=0: return np.array([],float)
    arr=np.asarray(arr,float).ravel()
    if arr.size != n:
        arr=resample_sequence(arr,n)
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
    S=float(arr.sum())
    if S<1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    return arr*(1.0/S)
```
Now generate:
```
def generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=24):
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None:
       pr=resample_sequence(parent,n)
       a=prepare_init(pr,n); inits.append(a)
       a=prepare_init(pr[::-1],n); inits.append(a)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    # uniform
    inits.append(prepare_init(np.ones(n),n))
    # exponentials
    for rate in (0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
       for base in (0.0,0.2,0.5):
          if len(inits)>=cap: break
          arr=np.exp(-rate*x)+base
          inits.append(prepare_init(arr,n))
          arr=np.exp(-rate*(1-x))+base
          inits.append(prepare_init(arr,n))
       if len(inits)>=cap: break
    # power
    for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       for base in (0.0,0.2,0.5):
          if len>=cap break
          arr=(1-x)**p + base
          inits.append(prepare_init(arr,n))
          arr=(x)**p + base
          inits.append(prepare_init(arr,n))
    # linear quadratics
    for slope in (-1.5,-0.8,-0.4,0.4,0.8,1.5):
       for curve in (-1.5,-0.5,0.0,0.5,1.5):
          if len>=cap break
          arr=1.0 + slope*(x-0.5) + curve*(x-0.5)**2
          inits.append(prepare_init(arr,n))
    # cos/sin
    for f in (1,2,3):
       for alpha in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
          if len>=cap break
          inits.append(prepare_init(1+alpha*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x), n))
          inits.append(prepare_init(1+alpha*np.sin(np.pi*f*x), n))
          inits.append(prepare_init(1+alpha*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x+0.7), n))
    # gaussians
    for c in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
       for w in (0.08,0.15,0.3):
          if len>=cap break
          inits.append(prepare_init(np.exp(-((x-c)/w)**2)+0.1, n))
    # random low-freq
    for _ in range(8):
       if len>=cap break
       noise=low_freq_noise(n, rng, int(np.log2(max(8,n))))? maybe max_freq=20
       arr=1.0 + 0.5*noise
       inits.append(prepare_init(arr,n))
    # If parent, multiplicative perturbation
    if parent is not None and len< cap:
       base=prepare_init(resample_sequence(parent,n),n)
       noise=low_freq_noise(n,rng,30); arr=base*np.exp(0.1*noise); inits.append(prepare_init(arr,n))
    # remove duplicates? not necessary. If len>cap: inits=inits[:cap]
    return inits[:cap]
```
Need `low_freq_noise` top-level.

`low_freq_noise(n, rng, max_freq=30)`:
```
N=n
spec=np.zeros(N//2+1, dtype=np.complex128)
mf=min(max_freq, N//2)
if mf>0:
    freqs=np.arange(1,mf+1)
    power=1.0/(freqs**1.5)
    amp=rng.normal(0, power)+1j*rng.normal(0,power)
    spec[freqs]=amp
# ensure Hermitian by irfft (it assumes one-sided)
noise=np.fft.irfft(spec, N)
m=noise.mean(); sd=noise.std()
if sd<1e-15: return np.zeros(N)
return (noise-m)/sd
```
Maybe max_freq 20-50. For small n 512, mf 20. Good.

In generate, `int(np.log2(max(8,n)))` for n=10240 ~13; maybe use 32. Use fixed 32.

Need if `cap` small, loops break. Use if len < cap before append; but above if break not perfect. We'll write a helper top-level `safe_append(inits, cap, a)`? No closure but can function:
```
def append_init(inits, cap, a):
   if len(inits) < cap:
      inits.append(a)
```
Top-level. Use it.

16. `perturb_best(a, rng, strength=None)`:
Use low_freq_noise.
```
def perturb_best(a, rng, strength=None):
   p=normalize_a(a); n=p.size
   if n==0: return p
   if strength is None: strength=float(rng.uniform(0.03,0.2))
   mode=int(rng.integers(0,6))
   if mode==0:
      noise=low_freq_noise(n,rng,32)
      q=p*np.exp(strength*noise)
   elif mode==1:
      t=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.2))
      if rng.random()<0.5: other=normalize_a(p[::-1])
      else: x=np.linspace(0,1,n); other=prepare_init(np.exp(-rng.uniform(0.5,3)*x),n)
      q=(1-t)*p+t*other
   elif mode==2:
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-1)); length=int(rng.integers(max(4,n//20), max(5,min(n, n//10)+1)))
      q=p.copy(); q[start:start+length]*=(1+strength*rng.uniform(-1,1))
   elif mode==3:
      d,S=d_from_a(p)
      if d.size:
         w=active_weights_topk(d,int(rng.integers(1,4)))
         h=corr_pair(p,w); hm=max(h.max(),1e-15)
         q=p*np.exp(-strength*0.5*h/hm)
      else: q=p.copy()
   elif mode==4:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      shape=prepare_init(np.exp(-rng.uniform(0.5,3)*x)+0.1,n)
      q=(1-0.2)*p+0.2*shape
   else:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      shape=1+strength*0.5*np.abs(2*x-1)
      q=p*shape
   q=np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
   return normalize_a(np.maximum(q,0.0))
```
Need `active_weights_topk` defined before perturb? Python top-level order not issue as long defined before call at runtime. We'll define active before perturb.

17. Active weight functions.

`active_weights_topk(d,k)`:
```
def active_weights_topk(d,k):
   d=np.asarray(d,float); m=d.size
   if m==0: return np.empty(0)
   if k<=0 or k>=m: return np.ones(m)/m
   if k==1:
      idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(d))])
   else:
      idx=np.argpartition(d, m-k)[-k:]
   w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1.0/float(k); return w
```
If d has nan, argpartition bad. Use `d=np.nan_to_num(d, nan=-1e100)`. Fine.

`active_weights_tolerance(d, tol, max_k)`:
```
def active_weights_tolerance(d, tol, max_k):
   d=np.nan_to_num(d,float)
   m=d.size; if m==0 return empty
   dmax=float(np.max(d)); mask=d >= dmax - tol
   idx=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
   if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(d))])
   elif idx.size>max_k:
       idx=np.argpartition(d, m-max_k)[-max_k:]
   w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1.0/idx.size; return w
```
If dmax negative? okay.

`active_weights_softmax(d,beta)`:
```
z=beta*(d-dmax); clip; e; sum; if sum<=0 uniform; return
```

```
def is_duplicate(a, existing, tol=1e-8):
   for b in existing:
      if b.size != a.size: continue
      diff=np.abs(a-b[:a.size]).max()
      if diff < tol: return True
   return False
```

Let's detail `low_freq_noise`:
```
def low_freq_noise(n, rng, max_freq=32):
    n=int(max(1,n))
    spec=np.zeros(n//2+1, dtype=np.complex128)
    mf=int(min(max_freq, n//2))
    if mf > 0:
        freqs=np.arange(1, mf+1, dtype=np.float64)
        power=1.0/(freqs**1.3)
        real=rng.normal(0.0, power)
        imag=rng.normal(0.0, power)
        spec[1:mf+1]=real + 1j*imag
    noise=np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
    noise = noise - float(np.mean(noise))
    sd=float(np.std(noise))
    if sd < 1e-15:
       return np.zeros(n)
    return noise/sd
```
If n=1, n//2=0, spec size1 zero, noise zero. Good.

`is_duplicate_array(a, existing, tol=1e-7)`:
```
def is_duplicate_array(a, existing, tol=1e-7):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    for b in existing:
       b=np.asarray(b,float).ravel()
       if b.size != a.size: continue
       if np.max(np.abs(a-b)) <= tol:
           return True
    return False
```
Could be O. Use.

Now, active weights indices:
```
def active_weights_indices(d, idx):
    m=d.size
    w=np.zeros(m)
    idx=np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(d))], dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        # Ensure only valid
        idx=idx[(idx>=0)&(idx<m)]
        if idx.size == 0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(d))])
    w[idx]=1.0/float(idx.size)
    return w
```
If idx has duplicates, setting same value okay but count not duplicates? If duplicates in top_idx? no. If tol idx unique. Good.

`mirror_log_polish`: Need call `exact_score_a` after each candidate. Could be many; check `deadline`. At top of outer loop check.

Let's code mirror carefully:
```
def mirror_log_polish(a_start, deadline, max_iters=100, k_list=(1,3,8), tol_list=(1e-4,), beta_w_list=(20,100), alpha_list=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2)):
    a=normalize_a(a_start)
    if a.size==0: return float('inf'), a
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score_a(a)
    cur_a=a.copy(); cur_sc=best_sc
    stale=0
    n=a.size
    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 5.0: break
        cur_a=normalize_a(cur_a)
        cur_sc=exact_score_a(cur_a)
        d,S=d_from_a(cur_a)
        if d.size == 0 or S <= 0.0: break
        dmax=float(np.max(d))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax): break
        maxk = max(int(max(k_list)), 32)
        maxk = min(maxk, d.size)
        if d.size > maxk:
            top_idx = np.argpartition(d, -maxk)[-maxk:]
        else:
            top_idx = np.argsort(d)[::-1]
        # sort top_idx descending by d
        if top_idx.size > 1:
            top_idx = top_idx[np.argsort(d[top_idx])[::-1]]
        weights=[]
        for k in k_list:
            kk=int(min(max(1,k), d.size))
            weights.append(active_weights_indices(d, top_idx[:kk]))
        for tol in tol_list:
            if len(weights) < 8:
                weights.append(active_weights_tolerance(d, float(tol), 64))
        for bv in beta_w_list:
            if len(weights) < 8:
                weights.append(active_weights_softmax(d, float(bv)))
        weights = weights[:6]
        improved_current=False
        improved_best=False
        for wi in range(len(weights)):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 3.0: break
            w=weights[wi]
            h=corr_pair(cur_a,w)
            LDS=float(np.dot(w,d))
            grad=(4.0*float(n)*h)/(S*S) - (4.0*LDS)/S
            ag=float(np.dot(cur_a,grad))
            p=grad - ag
            scale=float(np.max(np.abs(p)))
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-15: continue
            p=p/scale
            for alpha in alpha_list:
                if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.0: break
                q=cur_a * np.exp(-float(alpha)*p)
                q=normalize_a(np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0))
                sc=exact_score_a(q)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    if sc < cur_sc - 1e-12:
                        cur_sc=sc; cur_a=q.copy(); improved_current=True
                    if sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc=sc; best_a=q.copy(); improved_best=True
        if improved_best:
            stale=0
            print("mirror polish best {:.8f} iter {}".format(best_sc,it))
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale >= 5:
                break
    return float(exact_score_a(best_a)), best_a.copy()
```
If candidate improves current but not best, still continue alpha maybe. Good.
Note: `np.exp(-alpha*p)` with p scaled max abs 1, alpha up to 0.2; safe. Could use larger alpha in final? Include 0.03,0.08,0.2. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Algorithm:
- Work on the probability simplex using an anchored softmax parameterization.
- Smooth the max-convolution objective by a log-sum-exp (LSE) continuation,
  with FFT-based self-convolution and gradient/correlation.  For sparse
  active sets the correlation is computed directly instead of by FFT.
- Optimize the smoothed objective with L-BFGS-B.
- Multi-start from the previous solution when available, from a small-size
  global search, and from a bank of analytic/random smooth shapes.
- Polish with mirror-descent steps from exact active-set subgradients.
- Try a few nearby lengths and restarts near the deadline.
- Verify with the provided evaluate_sequence at the end.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, float(target) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(target) / s)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    m = int(a.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = next_power_of_two(m)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nfft)[:m]
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """
    Half-inner-product derivative vector h_i = sum_j w_{i+j} a_j.
    This is the gradient (up to the known factor) of weighted convolution
    coefficients with respect to a.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    wmax = float(np.max(np.abs(w)))
    if not np.isfinite(wmax) or wmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # Sparse path for small active sets: much faster in late polishing.
    thr = 1e-9 * wmax
    idx = np.nonzero(np.abs(w) > thr)[0]
    if idx.size > 0 and idx.size <= 64:
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for lag in idx:
            lag = int(lag)
            wt = float(w[lag])
            i0 = lag - (n - 1)
            if i0 < 0:
                i0 = 0
            i1 = lag
            if i1 > n - 1:
                i1 = n - 1
            if i0 > i1:
                continue
            j0 = lag - i0
            j1 = lag - i1
            j0 = int(min(j0, n - 1))
            j1 = int(max(j1, 0))
            if j0 < j1:
                continue
            h[i0:i1 + 1] += wt * a[j1:j0 + 1][::-1]
        return h

    # Dense FFT path.
    m = n + int(w.size) - 1
    nfft = next_power_of_two(m)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], nfft)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, nfft)
    full = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, nfft)[:m]
    return full[n - 1:2 * n - 1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def d_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64), s
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = float(n) * c / (s * s)
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
    return d, s


def exact_score_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))


def make_bounds(n, lo, hi):
    n = int(max(0, n))
    if n == 0:
        return []
    return [(float(lo), float(hi))] * n


def softmax_from_u(u):
    """Anchored softmax: z[0] = 0, z[1:] = u."""
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(u.size) + 1
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[0] = 0.0
    if u.size:
        z[1:] = u
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=80.0, neginf=-80.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -80.0, 80.0)
    m = float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def init_u_from_a(a):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a = a / float(a.sum())
    denom = float(a[0])
    if denom < 1e-300:
        denom = 1e-300
    u = np.log(a[1:] / denom)
    u = np.nan_to_num(u, nan=0.0, posinf=80.0, neginf=-80.0)
    return np.clip(u, -80.0, 80.0)


def lse_log_value_grad_u(u, beta):
    """
    Anchored-softmax LSE objective and gradient.
    u has dimension n-1, representing z=(0,u).
    """
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if u.size == 0:
        return 2.0, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    betaf = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(betaf) or betaf <= 1e-8:
        betaf = 100.0

    a = softmax_from_u(u)
    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(u.size, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = float(n) * c / (s * s)
    if d.size == 0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(u.size, dtype=np.float64)

    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(u.size, dtype=np.float64)

    z = betaf * (d - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(d.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(d.size)
        L = dmax
    else:
        w = e / se
        L = dmax + float(np.log(se)) / betaf

    h = corr_pair(a, w)
    lds = float(np.dot(w, d))
    score = 2.0 * L

    # Gradient of 2*sum(w*d) with respect to a (unrestrained).
    grad = (4.0 * float(n) * h) / (s * s)
    grad -= (4.0 * lds) / s

    # Project to simplex / softmax tangent space.
    ag = float(np.dot(a, grad))
    gz = a * (grad - ag)
    g_u = gz[1:]
    g_u = np.nan_to_num(g_u, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    g_u = np.clip(g_u, -1e8, 1e8)
    return float(score), g_u


def refine_with_beta(a, beta, deadline, maxfun, label=""):
    """Run repeated L-BFGS-B calls on the LSE objective at fixed beta."""
    a = normalize_a(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)

    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return a, best_a, best_sc

    u = init_u_from_a(a)
    bounds = make_bounds(u.size, -80.0, 80.0)
    maxfun = int(max(64, min(4000, maxfun)))
    beta = float(beta)

    cps = 80.0
    stall = 0

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = float(deadline - now)
        if rem < 5.0:
            break

        mf = int(min(maxfun, max(64, min(700, int(cps * max(1.0, rem) * 0.5)))))
        opts = {
            "ftol": 1e-16,
            "gtol": 1e-12,
            "maxiter": mf,
            "maxfun": mf,
            "maxls": 20,
            "maxcor": 30,
        }

        tcall = time.monotonic()
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_log_value_grad_u,
                u,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                args=(beta,),
                bounds=bounds,
                options=opts,
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        dt = time.monotonic() - tcall
        nfev = int(getattr(res, "nfev", mf) or mf)
        if dt > 1e-4:
            rate = float(max(1, nfev)) / float(dt)
            cps = float(max(10.0, min(2000.0, 0.6 * cps + 0.4 * rate)))
        else:
            cps = float(min(2000.0, cps * 1.3))

        u_new = np.asarray(getattr(res, "x", u), dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if u_new.size != u.size or not np.all(np.isfinite(u_new)):
            break

        a_new = softmax_from_u(u_new)
        a_new = normalize_a(a_new)
        sc = exact_score_a(a_new)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            stall = 0
            if label:
                print(label + " beta={:.4g} exact={:.8f}".format(beta, best_sc))
        else:
            stall += 1

        u = u_new
        a = a_new

        status = int(getattr(res, "status", 0))
        success = bool(getattr(res, "success", False))
        if success or status == 1:
            break
        if stall >= 6:
            break

    return a, best_a, float(best_sc)


def optimize_log_lbfgs(a_start, deadline, stages, maxfun, label=""):
    """
    LSE continuation. stages = [(beta, fraction_of_total_time), ...].
    """
    a = normalize_a(a_start)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = float(deadline - t0)
    if total < 8.0:
        return float(best_sc), best_a.copy()

    cum = 0.0
    for beta, frac in stages:
        cum += float(frac)
        sub_dead = t0 + cum * total
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(deadline) - 2.0)
        if sub_dead <= time.monotonic() + 3.0:
            break

        a_cur, best1, best1_sc = refine_with_beta(a, float(beta), sub_dead, maxfun, label)
        if np.isfinite(best1_sc) and best1_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(best1_sc)
            best_a = best1.copy()

        if np.isfinite(best1_sc):
            a = normalize_a(a_cur)
        else:
            a = normalize_a(best_a)

    return float(exact_score_a(best_a)), best_a.copy()


def active_weights_topk(d, k):
    d = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=-1e100, posinf=1e100, neginf=-1e100)
    m = int(d.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(max(1, min(k, m)))
    if k >= m:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    if k == 1:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(d))], dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(d, m - k)[-(k):].astype(np.int64)
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def active_weights_indices(d, idx):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(d.size)
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(d))], dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = idx[(idx >= 0) & (idx < m)]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(d))], dtype=np.int64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
    return w


def active_weights_tolerance(d, tol, max_k):
    d = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=-1e100, posinf=1e100, neginf=-1e100)
    m = int(d.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return active_weights_topk(d, 1)

    mask = d >= dmax - float(tol)
    idx = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    max_k = int(max(1, min(max_k, m)))
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(d))], dtype=np.int64)
    elif idx.size > max_k:
        idx = np.argpartition(d, m - max_k)[-(max_k):].astype(np.int64)

    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
    return w


def active_weights_softmax(d, beta):
    d = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=-1e100, posinf=1e100, neginf=-1e100)
    m = int(d.size)
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    beta = float(max(1e-6, beta))
    z = beta * (d - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return e / s


def mirror_log_polish(a_start, deadline, max_iters=100, k_list=(1, 3, 8),
                      tol_list=(1e-4,), beta_w_list=(20, 100),
                      alpha_list=(0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2)):
    """
    Mirror descent on the simplex using exact subgradients from a small set
    of active convolution lags. Updates log(a) multiplicatively.
    """
    a = normalize_a(a_start)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)
    cur_a = a.copy()
    cur_sc = best_sc
    n = int(a.size)
    stale = 0

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 5.0:
            break

        cur_a = normalize_a(cur_a)
        cur_sc = exact_score_a(cur_a)
        d, s = d_from_a(cur_a)
        if d.size == 0 or s <= 0.0:
            break

        dmax = float(np.max(d))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax):
            break

        maxk = 32
        for k in k_list:
            maxk = max(maxk, int(k))
        maxk = int(min(maxk, d.size))

        if d.size > maxk:
            top_idx = np.argpartition(d, -maxk)[-(maxk):].astype(np.int64)
        else:
            top_idx = np.argsort(d)[::-1].astype(np.int64)

        if top_idx.size > 1:
            top_idx = top_idx[np.argsort(d[top_idx])[::-1]]

        weights = []
        for k in k_list:
            kk = int(max(1, min(int(k), d.size)))
            weights.append(active_weights_indices(d, top_idx[:kk]))
        for tol in tol_list:
            if len(weights) < 8:
                weights.append(active_weights_tolerance(d, float(tol), 64))
        for bv in beta_w_list:
            if len(weights) < 8:
                weights.append(active_weights_softmax(d, float(bv)))
        weights = weights[:6]

        improved_best = False

        for wi in range(len(weights)):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 2.0:
                break
            w = weights[wi]
            h = corr_pair(cur_a, w)
            lds = float(np.dot(w, d))
            grad = (4.0 * float(n) * h) / (s * s)
            grad -= (4.0 * lds) / s

            ag = float(np.dot(cur_a, grad))
            p = grad - ag
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(p))) if p.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-15:
                continue
            p = p / scale

            for alpha in alpha_list:
                if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.0:
                    break
                q = cur_a * np.exp(-float(alpha) * p)
                q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                q = normalize_a(q)
                sc = exact_score_a(q)
                if not np.isfinite(sc):
                    continue
                if sc < cur_sc - 1e-12:
                    cur_sc = float(sc)
                    cur_a = q.copy()
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_a = q.copy()
                    improved_best = True

        if improved_best:
            stale = 0
            print("mirror polish best={:.8f} iter={}".format(best_sc, it))
        else:
            stale += 1
            if stale >= 5:
                break

    return float(exact_score_a(best_a)), best_a.copy()


def low_freq_noise(n, rng, max_freq=32):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    spec = np.zeros(n // 2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)
    mf = int(min(max_freq, n // 2))
    if mf > 0:
        freqs = np.arange(1, mf + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        power = 1.0 / (freqs ** 1.3)
        real = rng.normal(0.0, power)
        imag = rng.normal(0.0, power)
        spec[1:mf + 1] = real + 1j * imag
    noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
    noise = noise - float(np.mean(noise))
    sd = float(np.std(noise))
    if sd < 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return (noise / sd).astype(np.float64)


def prepare_init(arr, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    arr = resample_sequence(arr, n)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return arr * (1.0 / s)


def append_init(lst, cap, a):
    if len(lst) < int(cap):
        lst.append(a)


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=24):
    inits = []
    cap = int(max(1, cap))

    if parent is not None:
        pr = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(pr, n))
        append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(pr[::-1], n))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n))

    # Skewed exponentials, with optional floor.
    for rate in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        for base in (0.0, 0.2, 0.5):
            append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(np.exp(-rate * x) + base, n))
            append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)) + base, n))
            if len(inits) >= cap:
                break
        if len(inits) >= cap:
            break

    # Power edges.
    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        for base in (0.0, 0.2, 0.5):
            append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init((1.0 - x) ** p + base, n))
            append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(x ** p + base, n))
            if len(inits) >= cap:
                break
        if len(inits) >= cap:
            break

    # Low-order polynomials.
    for slope in (-1.5, -0.8, -0.4, 0.4, 0.8, 1.5):
        for curve in (-1.5, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.5):
            t = x - 0.5
            arr = 1.0 + slope * t + curve * t * t
            append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(arr, n))
            if len(inits) >= cap:
                break
        if len(inits) >= cap:
            break

    # Trig shapes.
    for f in (1, 2, 3):
        for alpha in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
            append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(1.0 + alpha * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x), n))
            append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(1.0 + alpha * np.sin(np.pi * f * x), n))
            append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(1.0 + alpha * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + 0.7), n))
            if len(inits) >= cap:
                break
        if len(inits) >= cap:
            break

    # Gaussians.
    for c in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
        for wgt in (0.08, 0.15, 0.30):
            arr = np.exp(-(((x - c) / wgt) ** 2)) + 0.1
            append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(arr, n))
            if len(inits) >= cap:
                break
        if len(inits) >= cap:
            break

    # Smooth random shapes.
    for _ in range(8):
        if len(inits) >= cap:
            break
        noise = low_freq_noise(n, rng, 32)
        append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(1.0 + 0.5 * noise, n))

    # Multiplicative perturbation of parent, if present.
    if parent is not None and len(inits) < cap:
        base = prepare_init(resample_sequence(parent, n), n)
        noise = low_freq_noise(n, rng, 40)
        append_init(inits, cap, prepare_init(base * np.exp(0.12 * noise), n))

    return inits[:cap]


def is_duplicate_array(a, existing, tol=1e-7):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    for b in existing:
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if b.size != a.size:
            continue
        if float(np.max(np.abs(a - b))) <= tol:
            return True
    return False


def quick_small_search(parent, n_small, deadline, n_out, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    inits = generate_inits(int(n_small), rng, parent, cap=20)

    scored = []
    for idx, a in enumerate(inits):
        a = normalize_a(a)
        scored.append((exact_score_a(a), idx, a))
    scored.sort()

    n_select = int(min(max(2, 2 * n_out), len(scored)))
    top = [scored[i][2] for i in range(n_select)]

    results = []
    total = float(max(1.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
    if len(top) == 0 or total < 20.0:
        return []

    per = total / float(len(top))
    small_stages = [
        (50.0, 0.15),
        (200.0, 0.25),
        (800.0, 0.30),
        (4000.0, 0.20),
        (16000.0, 0.10),
    ]

    for idx, a in enumerate(top):
        sub = time.monotonic() + per - 3.0
        sub = min(float(sub), float(deadline) - 3.0)
        sc, seq = optimize_log_lbfgs(a, sub, small_stages, maxfun=200, label="small{}:".format(idx))
        results.append((sc, idx, seq))

    results.sort()
    return [r[2] for r in results[:n_out]]


def perturb_best(a, rng, strength=None):
    p = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.18))

    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

    if mode == 0:
        noise = low_freq_noise(n, rng, 40)
        q = p * np.exp(strength * noise)

    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.20))
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            other = normalize_a(p[::-1])
        else:
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            rate = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0))
            other = prepare_init(np.exp(-rate * x) + 0.1, n)
        q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * other

    elif mode == 2:
        length = int(rng.integers(max(4, n // 50), max(5, min(n, n // 8) + 1)))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - length)))
        q = p.copy()
        q[start:start + length] *= (1.0 + strength * float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)))

    elif mode == 3:
        d, s = d_from_a(p)
        if d.size:
            k = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
            w = active_weights_topk(d, k)
            h = corr_pair(p, w)
            hm = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
            if hm > 1e-15:
                q = p * np.exp(-0.5 * strength * h / hm)
            else:
                q = p.copy()
        else:
            q = p.copy()

    elif mode == 4:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        rate = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 3.0))
        other = prepare_init(np.exp(-rate * x) + 0.1, n)
        q = 0.8 * p + 0.2 * other

    else:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        shape = 1.0 + 0.5 * strength * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)
        q = p * shape

    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return normalize_a(np.maximum(q, 0.0))


def seq_to_list(a):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def verify_list(lst):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + total_budget - 30.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load previous construction if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 16 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Main length: 10240 keeps both self-conv and correlation FFT sizes at 32768.
    n_primary = 10240

    print("parent_available={} parent_len={}".format(
        parent is not None, parent.size if parent is not None else 0))

    best_seq = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    # Small-size global search to obtain shape prototypes.
    small_seconds = 120.0 if parent is not None else 240.0
    small_end = t0 + min(float(small_seconds), 0.12 * total_budget)
    small_shapes = []
    if deadline - t0 > small_end - t0 + 120.0:
        small_shapes = quick_small_search(parent, 1024, small_end, 4, seed + 101)
        print("small search done t={:.1f}s shapes={}".format(time.monotonic() - t0, len(small_shapes)))

    # Build primary candidate bank.
    cand = generate_inits(n_primary, rng, parent, cap=24)
    for s in small_shapes:
        cand.append(prepare_init(resample_sequence(s, n_primary), n_primary))

    if parent is not None:
        cand.append(prepare_init(resample_sequence(parent, n_primary), n_primary))
        cand.append(prepare_init(resample_sequence(parent[::-1], n_primary), n_primary))

    scored = []
    for idx, a in enumerate(cand):
        a = normalize_a(a)
        sc = exact_score_a(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_seq = a.copy()
        scored.append((sc, idx, a))
    scored.sort()

    if best_seq is None and scored:
        best_seq = scored[0][2]
        best_sc = float(scored[0][0])
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = normalize_a(np.ones(n_primary, dtype=np.float64))
        best_sc = exact_score_a(best_seq)

    # Ordered unique starts: parent first if available, then best initial shapes.
    ordered = []
    if parent is not None:
        ordered.append(prepare_init(resample_sequence(parent, n_primary), n_primary))
        ordered.append(prepare_init(resample_sequence(parent[::-1], n_primary), n_primary))
    for sc, idx, a in scored:
        ordered.append(a)

    starts = []
    for arr in ordered:
        arr = normalize_a(arr)
        if not is_duplicate_array(arr, starts, 1e-7):
            starts.append(arr)
        if len(starts) >= 8:
            break

    if not starts:
        starts = [best_seq]

    print("initial best={:.8f} nstarts={}".format(best_sc, len(starts)))

    # Primary LSE continuation.
    primary_stages = [
        (20.0, 0.05),
        (80.0, 0.10),
        (300.0, 0.15),
        (1000.0, 0.20),
        (4000.0, 0.18),
        (15000.0, 0.17),
        (80000.0, 0.15),
    ]

    main_end = t0 + 0.62 * total_budget
    if main_end < time.monotonic() + 90.0:
        main_end = deadline - 90.0
    main_end = min(float(main_end), float(deadline) - 300.0)

    fracs = (0.24, 0.16, 0.13, 0.11, 0.09, 0.08, 0.07, 0.12)

    for idx, start_arr in enumerate(starts):
        if idx >= len(fracs):
            break
        if time.monotonic() > main_end - 20.0:
            break

        rem_main = max(0.0, float(main_end - time.monotonic()))
        rem_sum = 0.0
        for j in range(idx, len(fracs)):
            rem_sum += fracs[j]
        if rem_sum <= 0 or rem_main < 30.0:
            break
        chunk = rem_main * fracs[idx] / rem_sum
        sub_dead = time.monotonic() + chunk
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(main_end) - 5.0)
        if sub_dead <= time.monotonic() + 15.0:
            continue

        sc, seq = optimize_log_lbfgs(start_arr, sub_dead, primary_stages, maxfun=700,
                                     label="start{}:".format(idx))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print("primary start{} improved best={:.8f}".format(idx, best_sc))

    # A couple of perturbation restarts, then long exact-active mirror polish.
    polish_end = min(float(deadline) - 120.0, t0 + 0.88 * total_budget)
    perturb_end = min(float(polish_end) - 150.0, t0 + 0.80 * total_budget)

    perturb_stages = [
        (2000.0, 0.50),
        (20000.0, 0.50),
    ]

    perturb_count = 0
    while time.monotonic() < perturb_end - 70.0 and perturb_count < 3:
        left = max(0.0, float(perturb_end - 70.0 - time.monotonic()))
        remaining = max(1, 3 - perturb_count)
        chunk = max(20.0, left / float(remaining) - 10.0)
        chunk = min(chunk, 160.0)
        sub_dead = time.monotonic() + chunk
        if sub_dead > perturb_end - 20.0:
            sub_dead = max(time.monotonic() + 30.0, perturb_end - 20.0)

        p = perturb_best(best_seq, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.15)))
        sc1, seq1 = optimize_log_lbfgs(p, sub_dead, perturb_stages, maxfun=350,
                                       label="perturb{}:".format(perturb_count))
        sub_polish = min(float(deadline) - 60.0, sub_dead + 30.0)
        sc2, seq2 = mirror_log_polish(seq1, sub_polish, max_iters=25,
                                      k_list=(1, 2, 3, 5),
                                      tol_list=(5e-5,),
                                      beta_w_list=(30,),
                                      alpha_list=(0.02, 0.06, 0.15))
        for sc, seq in ((sc1, seq1), (sc2, seq2)):
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_seq = seq.copy()
                print("perturb{} found best={:.8f}".format(perturb_count, best_sc))
        perturb_count += 1

    # Long polishing of the current best.
    sc, seq = mirror_log_polish(
        best_seq, polish_end,
        max_iters=250,
        k_list=(1, 2, 3, 5, 8),
        tol_list=(3e-5, 1e-4),
        beta_w_list=(20, 80),
        alpha_list=(0.01, 0.03, 0.08, 0.20),
    )
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = float(sc)
        best_seq = seq.copy()
        print("long mirror polish best={:.8f}".format(best_sc))

    # Final length exploration: 8192 is cheap and can sometimes give a different optimum.
    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 180.0:
        trial_lengths = []
        if n_primary != 8192:
            trial_lengths.append(8192)
        if n_primary != 10240:
            trial_lengths.append(10240)

        for L in trial_lengths[:2]:
            if deadline - time.monotonic() < 120.0:
                break
            rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0)
            chunk = max(30.0, rem / 2.0 - 10.0)
            sub_dead = time.monotonic() + min(chunk, 240.0)
            a = prepare_init(resample_sequence(best_seq, L), L)

            quick_stages = [
                (1000.0, 0.50),
                (20000.0, 0.50),
            ]
            sc1, seq1 = optimize_log_lbfgs(a, sub_dead, quick_stages, maxfun=400,
                                           label="len{}:".format(L))
            sub_polish = min(float(deadline) - 30.0, sub_dead + 40.0)
            sc2, seq2 = mirror_log_polish(seq1, sub_polish, max_iters=40,
                                          k_list=(1, 2, 3, 5),
                                          tol_list=(5e-5,),
                                          beta_w_list=(30,),
                                          alpha_list=(0.02, 0.08, 0.20))
            for sc, seq in ((sc1, seq1), (sc2, seq2)):
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_seq = seq.copy()
                    print("length {} improved best={:.8f}".format(L, best_sc))

    # Final tiny cleanup.
    final_dead = max(time.monotonic() + 10.0, deadline - 8.0)
    sc, seq = mirror_log_polish(
        best_seq, final_dead,
        max_iters=30,
        k_list=(1, 2, 3),
        tol_list=(5e-5,),
        beta_w_list=(30,),
        alpha_list=(0.01, 0.04, 0.1),
    )
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = float(sc)
        best_seq = seq.copy()

    # Build output and compare with previous parent via the actual evaluator if possible.
    final_candidates = []

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(10240, dtype=np.float64) / 10240.0
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / s
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    final_candidates.append(seq_to_list(out_arr))

    if parent is not None:
        p_arr = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n_primary))
        p_arr = np.nan_to_num(p_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        p_arr = np.clip(p_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        final_candidates.append(seq_to_list(p_arr))

    best_final = final_candidates[0]
    best_final_score = verify_list(best_final)

    for lst in final_candidates[1:]:
        v = verify_list(lst)
        if (np.isfinite(v) and (not np.isfinite(best_final_score) or v < best_final_score)):
            best_final_score = v
            best_final = lst

    print("FINAL internal_bound={:.8f} verified_best={:.8f} length={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        best_sc, best_final_score if np.isfinite(best_final_score) else float("nan"),
        len(best_final), time.monotonic() - t0))

    # Absolute fallback: if verification failed, return a valid normalized sequence.
    if not best_final or len(best_final) == 0:
        best_final = seq_to_list(normalize_a(np.ones(10240, dtype=np.float64)))

    return best_final
```
